ch03-010

High-YieldSplit Duty & Combined Absence — DA Calculation Rule

ExceptionExplanation
Source L1106–1126
Exact-Source Fact Card

Under the TA Rules, Daily Allowance is calculated on a calendar-day (midnight-to-midnight) basis. This means that if an official is required to leave headquarters for a task, return mid-day, and then go out again the same day (a "split duty" scenario), the total absence from headquarters within that single calendar day is aggregated for DA calculation:

  • Split Duty Example: An official leaves HQ at 9:00 AM, returns at 1:00 PM (4 hours absent), then goes out again at 3:00 PM and returns at 7:00 PM (4 hours absent). Total absence on that calendar day = 4 + 4 = 8 hours. This falls in the 6–12 hours band → 70% DA is payable.
  • Key Principle: The periods are not treated as two separate journeys each attracting 30% DA. Instead, the combined total absence determines the applicable percentage band for the entire day.
  • Local-journey scope: The signed 12.09.2018 letter gives the 15/35/50 food-DA scale for qualifying local journeys (temporary duty point beyond 8 km from normal place of duty). It does not address split-trip aggregation; do not extend the ordinary-tour aggregation statement to local journeys without the controlling current SR/GIO.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Split Duty kya hoti hai? Imagine karo aapka office Delhi mein hai. Subah 9 baje aapko Noida (25 km door) jaana pada kisi meeting ke liye. Dopahar 1 baje wapas aa gaye (4 ghante gaye). Phir shaam ko 3 baje dobara Gurugram (30 km door) jaana pada doosri meeting ke liye. Shaam 7 baje wapas aa gaye (phir se 4 ghante gaye). Ab sawal ye hai: DA kaise calculate hoga — dono trips ka alag-alag, ya ek saath?
  • Answer: Total jodo, phir band dekho! Rule ye hai ki ek calendar day (midnight se midnight) mein jitne bhi absences hain, unhe sabko add karo: 4 hours + 4 hours = 8 hours total absence. Ab 8 hours kaunse band mein aata hai? → 6-12 hours band → 70% DA. To Level 9 officer ko (food DA ₹1,125) → 70% × ₹1,125 = ₹787.50 milega us din ka.
  • Agar alag-alag treat karte to kya hota? Agar har trip ko alag treat karte: 4 hours = <6 hours band = 30% DA, phir 4 hours = <6 hours band = 30% DA. Total = 30% + 30% = 60%. Lekin rule combined calculation karta hai → 70% milta hai. So combined method employee ke favor mein hai — 10% zyada DA milta hai is example mein.
  • Local-journey exception: Signed DoE letter 12.09.2018 gives 15/35/50 Food DA for qualifying local journeys. It does not address split-trip aggregation, so the local exception in this split-duty example remains unresolved; confirm the current SR/GIO before applying it.
  • Practical tip: Apne TA bill mein split duty ke case mein total absence clearly mention karo — "Total absence on [date] = X hours" — taaki Accounts section sahi band mein calculate kare. Dono trips ke times separately likho lekin total bhi dikhao.
Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

Split duty: aggregate absence within the calendar day (4 h + 4 h = 8 h → 70%), not two 30% claims. Do not extend aggregation to local journeys without the current SR/GIO.

ch03-011Micro-note — Transit DA for Overnight Journeys — Calendar-Day Calculation When a train or fli…
Source L1127–1136

Transit DA for Overnight Journeys — Calendar-Day Calculation

When a train or flight spans across midnight, the DA for each calendar day is calculated separately based on the actual hours of absence on that specific day. This is not a "full day" just because you were travelling.

Worked Example: A Level 9 officer (Food DA ceiling: ₹1,125/day) boards a train departing at 8:00 AM on Day 1 and arrives at the destination at 2:00 AM on Day 2.

• Day 1: Absence from 8:00 AM to midnight (24:00) = 16 hours → >12 hours band → 100% DA = ₹1,125.
• Day 2: Absence from midnight (00:00) to 2:00 AM = 2 hours → <6 hours band → 30% DA = ₹337.50.
• Total DA for this transit: ₹1,125 + ₹337.50 = ₹1,462.50.

Key Insight: Day 2's absence is only 2 hours (midnight to 2 AM), NOT a "full travel day." Many candidates wrongly claim 100% DA for both days — a classic trap!

Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

Overnight travel: each calendar day computed separately (Day 1 16 h → 100% = ₹1,125; Day 2 2 h → 30% = ₹337.50; total ₹1,462.50 for Level 9).

ch03-012Micro-note — Duty Pass vs TA — Railway Employees' Special Rule Indian Railway employees are e…ProvisoException
Source L1137–1145

Duty Pass vs TA — Railway Employees' Special Rule

Indian Railway employees are entitled to Duty Passes (free rail travel on official duty) issued under Railway Board rules. This is a separate entitlement from the Travelling Allowance (TA) system covered in this guide.

• If a Railway employee uses a Duty Pass for a journey, they cannot simultaneously claim TA (travel fare) for that same leg of the journey.
• If a Railway employee is eligible for a Duty Pass but does not use it (e.g., travels by air because rail is not feasible), they cannot claim TA for that leg unless a specific relaxation is granted by the competent authority.
• Non-Railway Ministries: Employees of all other Central Government Ministries/Departments have no Duty Pass system — they claim TA as per the rules in this guide.

Exam Trap: "A Railway employee eligible for a Duty Pass chooses to travel by air on personal expense and claims TA." → Not admissible without specific relaxation.

Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

Railway employees: a Duty Pass leg cannot also earn TA; unused eligibility gives no TA without specific relaxation; non-Railway Ministries have no Duty Pass system.

ch04-004

Very High-YieldOwn car/taxi/scooter — default rates where no state rate exists

Source L1188–1201
Exact-Source Fact Card

At places where no specific rate has been prescribed either by the Directorate of Transport of the concerned State or of a neighbouring State, Mileage Allowance for road journeys by own car/taxi/auto-rickshaw/own scooter is payable at currently applicable (25%-enhanced) rates of ₹30 per km (own car/taxi) and ₹15 per km (auto/own scooter etc.). Where a state-specific Road Mileage Allowance (RMA) rate does exist (e.g., defence services use PCDA-notified state-wise RMA rates), that state rate governs instead.

ModeCurrent Rate (Applicable Today)
Own car / taxi₹30 per km
Auto-rickshaw / own scooter₹15 per km
Journey on foot₹15 per km (additional allowance)
Trap note: "on foot" and "auto/scooter" share the same default rate (₹15/km) — easy to mix up in MCQs, but they are conceptually different entitlements (one is a mode of local travel reimbursement, the other a foot-travel-specific add-on).
Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

Where neither the State nor a neighbouring State Directorate of Transport has prescribed a rate: own car/taxi ₹30/km; auto-rickshaw/own scooter ₹15/km; on foot ₹15/km (25%-enhanced rates). A state-specific RMA rate (e.g. PCDA state-wise, for defence) governs instead.

ch04-005Micro-note — The "First Kilometre" Rule — Dead Kilometre for Road Mileage Under the FR/SR rul…Exception
Source L1202–1212

The "First Kilometre" Rule — Dead Kilometre for Road Mileage

Under the FR/SR rules (carry-over from the 6th CPC regime, still applicable in most departments), no mileage is payable for the first kilometre of a road journey performed by own car or hired taxi. The calculation of Mileage Allowance begins from the 2nd kilometre onward.

Worked Example: An officer travels 10 km by own car for an official tour.
• Billable distance: 10 − 1 = 9 km (the first km is a "dead kilometre").
• Mileage Allowance: 9 km × ₹30/km = ₹270 (NOT ₹300).
• Trap: If the question says "10 km by own car at ₹30/km" — don't blindly compute 10 × 30 = ₹300. Deduct the dead kilometre first!

Exception: This rule applies to own car and hired taxi journeys. For journeys by auto-rickshaw (metered fare) or public bus, the actual fare paid is reimbursed without this deduction.

Exam Note: While the Department of Expenditure's 2017 O.M. does not explicitly restate the dead-km rule in its main text, it continues to be applied by most Ministries' Accounts sections as an inherited FR/SR practice. Verify with your Ministry's internal TA manual for departmental applicability.

Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

Dead-km rule (inherited FR/SR practice, not restated in the 2017 O.M. main text): no mileage for the 1st km by own car/hired taxi — 10 km × ₹30 → 9 × 30 = ₹270. Metered auto/public bus: actual fare, no deduction. Verify against the Ministry’s TA manual.

Study aid · Memory hook

“Ten km, nine paid” — drop the dead kilometre before multiplying.

Official-text check · read from the official PDF on 26.11.2026

No dead-kilometre deduction appears in para 2D of the 13.07.2017 O.M. (E3). Para 2D(i) road entitlements by level (AC taxi/bus rules) are missing from this chapter: S4.

ch04-007Worked example — Worked Example Scenario: A Level 6 officer travels 40 km by own car in a state w…
Source L1228–1236
Worked Example

Scenario: A Level 6 officer travels 40 km by own car in a state with no Directorate-of-Transport-notified rate, on official tour.
Given: The current road mileage rate is ₹30/km.
Step 1: Identify applicable rate — no state rate exists, so use the default.
Step 2: Distance × rate = 40 × ₹30 = ₹1,200.
Result: ₹1,200 payable as Mileage Allowance for that road journey.
Counter-example: If this journey were in Punjab (state RMA ₹15/km, illustrative defence table), the state-specific rate would override the default — always check for a state rate first.

Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

Level 6, 40 km own car, no state rate → 40 × ₹30 = ₹1,200 (as the source’s worked example states it, without deducting the dead km); always check for a state rate first.

Chapter 5 — TA on Transfer · source lines 1258–1402

TA on Transfer

Chapter study mapPARTIAL / OPEN

ch05-004

Very High-YieldComposite Transfer Grant (CTG)

ProvisoException
Source L1270–1284
Exact-Source Fact Card

Composite Transfer Grant is payable at 80% of the last month's basic pay for an eligible transfer involving a change of station located 20 km or more apart. For a transfer below 20 km or within the same city/station, the 13.07.2017 O.M. allows one-third of the normal CTG only if an actual change of residence is involved; otherwise CTG is nil. For transfer to/from the Island territories of Andaman & Nicobar and Lakshadweep, CTG is paid at the enhanced rate of 100% of last month's basic pay. Where both spouses (both Central Govt servants) are transferred from the same station to the same station within 6 months — but the second transfer is after 60 days of the first — full CTG is paid to the spouse transferred earlier and 50% CTG to the spouse transferred later. CTG on retirement was separately clarified admissible vide O.M. dated 06.01.2022.

ScenarioCTG rate
Transfer ≥ 20 km, general case80% of last month's basic pay
Transfer < 20 km / same cityOne-third of normal CTG if an actual residence change occurs; otherwise nil
Transfer to/from A&N / Lakshadweep100% of last month's basic pay
Both spouses transferred, same O-D, within 6 months (2nd after 60 days)Full CTG to earlier-transferred spouse; 50% to later-transferred spouse
RED-FLAG TRAP

Flag: "CTG is always 80% regardless of distance." Fix: For an eligible transfer at 20 km or more, ordinary CTG is 80%; below 20 km or within the same city/station, one-third of normal CTG applies only if an actual residence change occurs, otherwise CTG is nil. Specified A&N/Lakshadweep transfers have the 100% rate. Fix-phrase: "Below 20 km: residence change matters; the islands have a separate 100% rate."

Official-text check · read from the official PDF on 26.11.2026

Official wording: “Composite Transfer and Packing Grant”, change of station “at a distance of or more than 20 kms”; NPA and MSP excluded from basic pay (E7). Transfers at own request / not in public interest remain governed by the earlier rules (para 3(ii)(c)). Transitional cases: S6.

ch05-005

Very High-Yield — source status openTransfer vs Tour — apply the order and current SR 114 conditions

OrderExceptionExplanation
Source L1285–1304
Primary-source boundary

Transfer TA and tour TA are distinct categories, but a duration-only “180-day switch” is not established by the primary text retrieved here. The official DoE O.M. dated 13.07.2017 says the general admissibility conditions prescribed in SR 114 continue to apply to transfer TA; it does not reproduce SR 114. The current complete Central SR 114 text, its applicable orders/amendments, and the exact transfer-order conditions remain unverified in this review.

  • When a transfer order is involved: Read its purpose, stated temporary/permanent nature and period, any extension/revision, station and residence facts, and the operative TA rule before deciding which components are admissible. If classed as a transfer, fare, CTG, personal-effects and conveyance components each retain their own conditions.
  • When duty is described as temporary or a tour: Do not infer the full TA package—or the absence of CTG/personal-effects transport—solely from the number of days. Apply the current rule and the order that actually authorized the movement.
  • Training is a separate question: Secondary transcriptions report training-specific options and schedules, but current SR 164/GIO text and their status are unresolved. Do not merge a training rule into a general SR 114 conclusion.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Transfer ya Tour — sirf din gin kar faisla nahi: Written order dekho: posting ka purpose/nature, approved period, extension/revision, station aur residence facts. Phir current SR 114 aur applicable instructions check karo. Is guide ne current full SR 114 text verify nahi kiya.
  • Example: 5 months (150 days) ka temporary-duty order apne aap “Tour, no CTG” prove nahi karta; 7 months bhi duration alone se poora transfer package prove nahi karta. Exact order aur operative rule ki zaroorat hai.
  • Training: SR 164 ke training rules alag hain; secondary references current primary text ka badal nahi. Is guide mein training-specific schedule/option bhi held hai.
  • Exam caution: Sirf duration di ho aur controlling order/conditions na diye hon, to answer ko settled key na samjho. Q21 isi wajah se unscored hai.
RED-FLAG TRAP

Flag: “Under 180 = Tour; over 180 = Transfer.” Fix: This duration-only shorthand was not verified in the current Central primary text retrieved. Do not classify a case or grant/deny CTG, baggage or family travel from duration alone; check the actual order and applicable SR 114 conditions.

ch05-006Micro-note — Same City / Urban Agglomeration (UA) — Transfer Within the Same Station What hap…
Source L1305–1316

Same City / Urban Agglomeration (UA) — Transfer Within the Same Station

What happens when an officer is moved from one office building to another within the same city (e.g., from North Block to South Block in Delhi, or from one campus to another in Bengaluru)?

When an officer is moved between offices in the same city or Urban Agglomeration (UA), keep the residence fact distinct from the station boundary:

  • No change of residence: If the move is within the same UA and does not involve an actual change of residence, the guide states no TA is payable (CTG nil; no fare reimbursement).
  • With change of residence within the same UA: The move is treated as a transfer within the same station. The cited rule gives one-third of normal CTG (1/3 × 80% = 26.67% of last basic pay), plus actual transportation charges for personal effects within the city.
  • Station boundary: The guide describes “station” for TA purposes as the entire Census-defined UA, not merely the municipal boundary; its example is Delhi UA including Noida, Gurugram, Faridabad and Ghaziabad.

Exam illustration: A North Block-to-South Block move (3 km) in Delhi with a residence change is shown as one-third CTG, not nil or full CTG, because both offices are within the same UA. Verify the case facts and applicable order before applying.

ch05-007

Very High-YieldTransfer TA — Spousal Scaling, Multiple Road Mileages, and Tax Reimbursements (SR 116)

ExceptionExplanation
Source L1318–1349
Exact-Source Fact Card

Under Swamy's Handbook-2024 and GIOs below SR 116, several critical administrative and financial nuances govern Travelling Allowance on Transfer:

  • 1. Multiple Road Mileages for Road Travel (GIO (5), Para IV, SR 116):
    If the transfer journey is performed between places not connected by rail otherwise than by public bus (e.g. by private car or hired vehicle), road mileage is payable at scaled rates based on the number of family members travelling:
    • One Road Mileage: For self, or self and one additional family member.
    • Two Road Mileages: If exactly two family members accompany the Government servant.
    • Three Road Mileages: If more than two family members accompany the Government servant.
  • 2. Reimbursement of Service Tax, GST, and Cess (GIO (10), SR 116):
    All elements of Service Tax, GST, and Education Cess charged by transport operators in the bill for the transportation of personal effects are fully reimbursable up to the employee's maximum entitlement ceiling.
  • 3. Conveyance Transport "Public Interest" Exemption (Item V, GIO (1), SR 116):
    For claiming the cost of transporting a personal vehicle (car/scooter) on transfer, the possession of the conveyance at the old or new station need not be in the public interest (reimbursable as long as it is owned strictly by the employee).
  • 4. Department of Posts Choice Posting Concession (SR 114):
    Under postal-specific GIOs, a posting to a place of choice on the completion of a full tenure is not treated as a transfer on own request. Consequently, such officials are fully entitled to Transfer TA and CTG under SR 114.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Transfer TA ke Swamy's Handbook ke 4 Special Rules (SR 116):
  • Rule 1 — Family ke count se mileage multiply hota hai: Agar aapka naya station train se connected nahi hai (jaise koi remote NE area) aur aapko private car/taxi se family ko le kar jaana hai, to road mileage family members ke count ke hisaab se badhti hai: Akele ya +1 member = 1× rate (₹30/km), 2 members = 2× rate (₹60/km), 3+ members = 3× rate (₹90/km). Example: 100 km journey, 3 family members → 100 × ₹90 = ₹9,000 mileage. Kyunki badi family ke liye badi gaadi chahiye (Innova jaisi), to zyada paisa lagta hai — sarkar ye consider karti hai.
  • Rule 2 — GST/Taxes fully reimbursable: Saman shift karne wale transporter ka bill mein jo GST, Service Tax, ya Education Cess laga hai — wo poora reimburse hoga (aapki entitlement limit ke andar). Example: Agar packing + transport bill ₹50,000 hai aur uspe ₹9,000 GST hai, to total ₹59,000 reimburse hoga (agar ye aapki weight/distance entitlement ke andar hai). Purane rules mein GST reimbursable nahi tha — ye 7th CPC ka improvement hai.
  • Rule 3 — Gaadi "public interest" mein hona zaroori nahi: Apni car ya scooter ko naye station pe train se bhejne ka paisa claim karne ke liye aapko ye prove nahi karna ki "ye gaadi sarkari duty ke liye zaroori hai." Bas gaadi aapke naam pe registered honi chahiye — bas. Chahe aap gaadi sirf weekend pe ghumne ke liye use karte ho — phir bhi transport charges milenge. Note: Gaadi spouse ke naam pe nahi honi chahiye — strictly employee ke naam pe.
  • Rule 4 — Post Office Choice Posting: Department of Posts (India Post) mein ek special rule hai: agar kisi postal employee ka tenure complete hone ke baad choice posting milti hai (jaise "aapne 5 saal Guwahati mein poore kiye, ab aapki pasand ka station — Lucknow — mil gaya"), to ise "own request transfer" nahi maana jaata. Iska matlab: full Transfer TA + full CTG milega. Normally own request transfer pe CTG kam milta hai — lekin ye exception hai.
ch05-008

High-YieldTransportation of personal effects (packing grant) & conveyance

Explanation
Source L1350–1363
Exact-Source Fact Card

Transportation of personal effects by road is reimbursed at currently applicable (25%-enhanced) rates of ₹62.50/km (Level 6 and above), ₹31.25/km (Level 5), and ₹18.75/km (Level 4 and below). By train/steamer, entitlement is by weight: Level 12 and above / Level 6–11 → 6,000 kg (by goods train/4-wheeler wagon/1 container — double container for Level 12+, single container for Level 6–11); Level 5 → 3,000 kg; Level 4 and below → 1,500 kg. Separately, transportation of a conveyance (motor car, motor cycle, scooter, moped, bicycle) is reimbursed: Level 6 and above may transport 1 motor car/similar or 1 motor cycle/scooter; Level 5 and below may transport 1 motorcycle/scooter/moped/bicycle (SR 116). Possession of the conveyance at the old or new station need not be shown to be in the public interest.

Pay LevelBy Train/SteamerBy Road (Current Rate)
12 and above6,000 kg (double container)₹62.50/km
6 to 116,000 kg (single container)₹62.50/km
53,000 kg₹31.25/km
4 and below1,500 kg₹18.75/km
Analogy

Think of the personal-effects entitlement like an airline baggage allowance that scales with "class of ticket" (Pay Level) — senior levels get a bigger "container," junior levels get a smaller weight cap.

ch05-009

Moderate-YieldSpecial-region transfers & TA entitlement basis on transfer

Source L1364–1371
Exact-Source Fact Card

In case of transfer from one post to another, TA entitlement is determined on the basis of the pay of the lower of the two posts/grades (i.e., old vs new). Special provisions for TA on transfer to/from the North-Eastern Region, Union Territories of Andaman & Nicobar, Lakshadweep Islands and Ladakh were clarified/enhanced vide O.M. No.19030/1/2017-E.IV dated 16.06.2022.

Memory Hook

"On transfer, you're paid like the junior of your two posts — never the senior."

ch05-012

Rapid-Revision Recap — Chapter 5

Proviso
Source L1390–1402
  • CTG: 80% for eligible transfers ≥20 km; below 20 km/same station, 1/3 of normal CTG only if residence actually changes, otherwise nil; 100% for the specified A&N/Lakshadweep transfers.
  • Spouse-transfer rule: full CTG to first-mover, 50% to second (within 6 months, gap >60 days).
  • Personal effects by road: ₹62.50/₹31.25/₹18.75 per km (Level 6+/5/≤4, Current).
  • By train: 6,000 kg (Level 6+), 3,000 kg (Level 5), 1,500 kg (Level ≤4).
  • TA on transfer = pay of the lower of old/new post.
  • Special enhanced provisions for NE Region/A&N/Lakshadweep/Ladakh (O.M. 16.06.2022).
  • Transfer vs Tour: Check the actual order and current SR 114 conditions; 180 days alone is not a verified universal classification rule.
  • Same UA transfer: No residence change → CTG Nil; With residence change → 1/3rd CTG (26.67% of Basic Pay).

ch06-004

Very High-YieldTime limits — general vs retirement vs death-in-service

O.M.Exception
Source L1416–1429
Exact-Source Fact Card

Ordinary tour/transfer/training TA claims must be submitted within 60 days succeeding the applicable trigger under GFR Rule 290 and DoE O.M. 13.03.2018, whether or not an advance was drawn; para 2 sets separate triggers for relevant family journeys and delivered personal effects/conveyances. TA on retirement has a distinct 180-day window under DoE O.M. 15.06.2021. These are claim-submission limits, not advance-adjustment deadlines. Where a Government servant dies in service, SR 148 separately addresses a family TA advance; do not import the ordinary tour-advance rules.

Claim typeTime limitReference
Ordinary TA claim submission (tour/transfer/training)60 days succeeding the applicable trigger, whether or not an advance was drawnGFR Rule 290; DoE O.M. 13.03.2018 (para 2 has separate triggers)
TA on Retirement180 daysO.M. 19030/1/2017-E.IV, 15.06.2021
Advance to family on death-in-serviceUp to 75% of likely TA claimSecondary compilation, cross-checked
RED-FLAG TRAP

Flag: "All TA claims must be filed within 60 days, no exceptions." Fix: Retirement TA claims get a specific relaxed window of 180 days — three times longer than the general rule, recognising the disruption of retirement. Fix-phrase: "60 is the rule, 180 is the retirement relief."

Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

Ordinary tour/transfer/training claims: 60 days succeeding the applicable trigger (GFR 290; O.M. 13.03.2018), advance or not. Retirement TA: 180 days (O.M. 15.06.2021). Neither is an advance-adjustment deadline. Death-in-service family advance: up to 75% of likely claim (secondary compilation, SR 148).

Study aid · Memory hook

“60 is the rule, 180 is the retirement relief.” (source fix-phrase)

Official-text check · read from the official PDF on 26.11.2026

Official GFR 290/291/295 and the 21.12.2016 O.M. (one-year limit extendable with FA approval): E8, E13.

ch06-005

Very High-YieldRetirement TA Exclusions & Station Boundaries (SR 147, SR 148)

ExceptionExplanation
Source L1431–1453
Exact-Source Fact Card

Under Swamy's Handbook-2024 and GIOs below SR 147 and SR 148, Travelling Allowance on Retirement is governed by strict boundary and eligibility rules:

  • 1. Strict Forfeiture on Resignation, Dismissal, or Removal:
    The retirement TA concession is admissible only to employees who retire on retiring pension, superannuation, invalid, or compensation pension.
    The Restriction: It is strictly NOT admissible to employees who quit service by resignation, or who are dismissed or removed from service as a disciplinary penalty.
  • 2. Last Station of Duty Boundary Definition:
    For the purpose of determining "same-station" or "change-of-residence" boundaries on transfer or retirement, the "last station of duty" strictly means and includes all areas falling within the Urban Agglomeration (UA) of that station. Moving within the UA without a change of residence does not attract CTG.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Retirement TA kis-kis ko milega aur kis-kis ko nahi? Ye bohot clearly defined hai — aur exams mein bohot test hota hai:
  • KO MILEGA (Eligible): (1) Superannuation pe retire hone wale (normal retirement at 60 years), (2) Retiring pension pe retire hone wale (voluntary retirement after qualifying service), (3) Invalid pension pe retire hone wale (medical grounds pe), (4) Compensation pension pe retire hone wale (post abolition etc.). Basically agar aapko pension mil rahi hai, to retirement TA bhi milega.
  • NAHI MILEGA (Not Eligible — Strictly): (1) Resignation (istifa) dene wale — chahe 20 saal service ho, agar resignation diya to retirement TA nahi milega. Reason: Resignation voluntary hai — aap khud chhod rahe ho, sarkar nahi nikaal rahi. (2) Dismissal ya Removal — disciplinary action se naukri se nikalne wale. (3) Temporary employees with <3 years service — agar temporary ho aur 3 saal se kam service hai, to nahi milega.
  • Station Boundary — Urban Agglomeration (UA): "Last station of duty" ka matlab sirf office ka building nahi — poori Urban Agglomeration shamil hai. Example: Agar aapka office Delhi ke North Block mein hai, to "last station" mein poora Delhi UA aata hai — Noida, Gurugram, Faridabad, Ghaziabad sab shamil hain. Agar aap retirement ke baad North Block se Gurugram shift hote ho (same UA mein), to CTG tab tak nahi milega jab tak actual ghar na badla ho.
  • Real-life scenario: Ek officer 30 saal service ke baad resign de deta hai (kisi private company mein jaane ke liye). Kya usko retirement TA milega? → Nahi! Resignation = no retirement TA, even with 30 years of service. Agar wahi officer superannuation pe retire hota (60 years complete karke), to full TA milta. Ye distinction bohot important hai.
Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

Retirement TA only for retiring pension, superannuation, invalid or compensation pension — NOT resignation, dismissal or removal. “Last station of duty” includes the whole Urban Agglomeration; moving within the UA without change of residence attracts no CTG.

ch06-006

High-YieldRe-employed persons & other retirement-adjacent clarifications

Proviso
Source L1454–1460
Exact-Source Fact Card

Concessions to persons re-employed in Central Government service regarding payment of TA were clarified vide O.M. No.19030/4/2020-E.IV dated 24.01.2022. TA/DA for Attendant/Escort accompanying a Government servant with disabilities during tour/training was separately provided for vide O.M. No.19030/3/2013-E.IV dated 17.02.2015. Government servants participating as players/athletes/managers/coaches/masseurs/doctors in sporting events of national/international importance within India are allowed to travel by Rail in First Class (or Economy Class by Air if travelling outside India), regardless of their ordinary pay-level entitlement.

Memory Hook

"R-A-S: Re-employed (2022), Attendant/Escort (2015), Sportspersons (First Class rail) — three special-category clarifications."

Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

Three special categories: re-employed persons (O.M. 19030/4/2020-E.IV, 24.01.2022); attendant/escort for employees with disabilities (O.M. 19030/3/2013-E.IV, 17.02.2015); sportspersons in national/international events in India — First Class rail (Economy air if outside India) regardless of pay level.

Study aid · Memory hook

R-A-S: Re-employed (2022), Attendant/Escort (2015), Sportspersons. (source hook)

ch06-007Micro-note — Revision of TA Bills — The 3-Year Limit (GFR Procedural Rule) After a TA bill ha…
Source L1461–1469

Revision of TA Bills — The 3-Year Limit (GFR Procedural Rule)

After a TA bill has been passed and payment made, it may still be revised under certain circumstances:

• Grounds for Revision: A passed TA bill can be revised for (a) arithmetic errors discovered post-payment, (b) subsequent increase in DA that retrospectively affects the rates applicable at the time of the journey, or (c) supplementary claims that were inadvertently omitted from the original bill.

• Time Limit: Under GFR provisions, a revised/supplementary TA claim can be processed within three (3) years from the date of the original claim. Beyond 3 years, no revision or supplementary claim is entertained.

• Supplementary Claim: An official who has already drawn TA on a bill may draw a supplementary claim even after the original bill has been passed — for example, if a DA rate revision occurred after the original bill was settled, or if a component was missed in the original claim.

Exam Trap: "A TA bill passed in March 2023 can be revised in June 2026 for a DA enhancement." → Yes, because 3 years and 3 months have passed, this would be time-barred. But if the revision is sought in February 2026 (within 3 years), it is admissible.

Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

Passed TA bill may be revised/supplemented for arithmetic errors, retrospective DA increase, or omitted components — within 3 years of the original claim (GFR procedural). [uncertain — see source line 1468: the exam-trap sentence answers “Yes” while calling the June 2026 revision time-barred.]

ch06-008Micro-note — TA for Pensioners / Retired Employees Called Back for Duty When a retired Govern…
Source L1470–1481

TA for Pensioners / Retired Employees Called Back for Duty

When a retired Government employee (pensioner) is summoned by a Central Government office — not for re-employment, but for specific purposes like:

• Testifying in a departmental inquiry or vigilance case,
• Appearing before a Medical Board for disability reassessment,
• Providing evidence or deposition in a court case related to their official service,

they are entitled to TA and DA as per their original pay level at the time of retirement for the duration of the visit. The travel fare (by the class they were entitled to at retirement) and Daily Allowance for the journey and halt days are reimbursable.

Important: This entitlement is distinct from re-employment (covered under O.M. 24.01.2022). A pensioner called for a one-off inquiry is not "re-employed" — they are summoned as a witness/deponent and are reimbursed accordingly.

Note: Pensioners do NOT get LTC (Leave Travel Concession) — LTC is a benefit for serving employees only.

Exam Trap: "A retired Level 11 officer is called to depose in a CBI inquiry. What TA do they get?" → Answer: TA/DA as per Level 11 (their retirement pay level), not as per any re-employment scale.

Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

Pensioner summoned (inquiry, medical board, court deposition) — not re-employment: TA/DA at the pay level at retirement; no LTC for pensioners.

Study aid · Practice MCQs (written for this guide; answers cite the source card)
Q1. Retirement TA claim window per the guide?
  1. 30 days
  2. 60 days
  3. 180 days
  4. One year

Answer: (c) 180 days — “TA on retirement has a distinct 180-day window under DoE O.M. 15.06.2021.” Rule citation: ch06-004 (L1416–1429)

Q2. Retirement TA is NOT admissible to an employee who:
  1. Retires on superannuation
  2. Retires on invalid pension
  3. Resigns
  4. Retires on compensation pension

Answer: (c) Resigns — Not admissible on resignation, dismissal or removal. Rule citation: ch06-005 (L1431–1453)

Q3. “Last station of duty” for same-station/change-of-residence purposes includes:
  1. Only the municipal limits
  2. The whole Urban Agglomeration
  3. Only the office campus
  4. A 20 km radius

Answer: (b) The whole Urban Agglomeration — “includes all areas falling within the Urban Agglomeration (UA) of that station.” Rule citation: ch06-005

Q4. Re-employed persons’ TA concession was clarified by which O.M.?
  1. 19030/4/2020-E.IV dated 24.01.2022
  2. 19030/3/2013-E.IV dated 17.02.2015
  3. 19030/1/2017-E.IV dated 13.07.2017
  4. 12(1)/2016-E.II(A)

Answer: (a) 19030/4/2020-E.IV dated 24.01.2022 — Fact Card, Re-employed persons. Rule citation: ch06-006 (L1454–1460)

Q5. Time limit for revising/supplementing a passed TA bill (GFR procedural provisions, as stated)?
  1. 6 months
  2. 1 year
  3. 3 years
  4. No limit

Answer: (c) 3 years — “within three (3) years from the date of the original claim.” Rule citation: ch06-007 (L1461–1469)

16.07.2026F.No. G-14019/02/2026-CashLTC-air booking reminder: 21-day lead encouraged; specified 30-day LTC-advance request for air-entitled employees.Government-hosted DGT reproduction checked; addressed to DoPT officers/officials. Direct DoPT copy not obtained; not a general TA/rail deadline.
29.10.2026No. 1/2(i)/2026-E.II(B)DA increased from 60% to 63%, effective 01.07.2026.Official DoE PDF checked; a current input, not whole-table validation.

ch07-006

Reference — status variesOM 1: DoE O.M. dated 04.09.2017 — Pay Level 13A Entitlement

O.M.Explanation
Source L1556–1576
Exact-Source Fact Card

Consequent upon the implementation of the 7th CPC, the newly introduced Pay Level 13A (carrying a pre-revised Grade Pay of ₹8,900, applicable to certain senior administrative grades like Directors/Directors-equivalent) was omitted from the baseline TA entitlements table of the 13.07.2017 O.M.

The Clarification: Department of Expenditure vide O.M. No. 19030/1/2017-E.IV dated 04.09.2017 clarified that the TA/DA travel and Daily Allowance entitlements of officers in Pay Level 13A are identical in all respects to those in Pay Level 13.

  • Travel Entitlement: Economy Class by Air, AC-I Class by Train.
  • Hotel Ceilings: Up to ₹5,625 per day.
  • Food Lump Sum: Up to ₹1,250 per day.
  • Local Travel within City: AC Taxi up to 50 km per day.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Level 13A ka confusion kyun hua? 7th CPC ne Pay Matrix mein ek naya level introduce kiya — Level 13A (Grade Pay ₹8,900 wala, jo Director-level posts ke liye hai). Lekin jab 13 July 2017 ko base TA O.M. nikla, to uski entitlement table mein Level 13A ka naam galti se chhut gaya — table mein sirf 14+, 12-13, 9-11, 6-8, aur 5- ye 5 bands the. Officers confuse ho gaye: "Hum Level 13A wale hain, to hume kya milega — Level 12 jaisa ya Level 13 jaisa?"
  • 4 September 2017 ko clarification aaya: Department of Expenditure ne ek short O.M. nikaal ke clear kar diya: Level 13A = Level 13 (same entitlements in all respects). Matlab Level 13A wale officers ko wo sab facilities milti hain jo Level 13 ko milti hain: Flight mein Economy Class, Train mein AC-I, Hotel ceiling ₹5,625/day (current, DA≥50% enhanced), Food lump sum ₹1,250/day, Local travel AC taxi up to 50 km/day.
  • Exam mein kaise puchhte hain? Examiners often puchhte hain: "Level 13A ki TA entitlement kya hai?" — Options mein Level 12 jaisa, Level 14 jaisa, Level 11 jaisa — ye sab traps hain. Correct answer: "Same as Level 13" — per O.M. dated 04.09.2017. Ye date aur ye fact dono yaad rakhna zaroori hai.
Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

OM 1 (04.09.2017): Level 13A = Level 13 in all respects — Economy air, AC-I, hotel ₹5,625, food ₹1,250, AC taxi up to 50 km/day.

Official-text check · read from the official PDF on 26.11.2026

Official basis: Level 13A included in the Pay Matrix by GSR 592(E) dated 15.06.2017; O.M. 04.09.2017 makes 13A equivalent to Level 13 (verified).

ch07-007

Reference — status variesOM 2: DoE O.M. dated 14.09.2017 — TA/DA of Non-Officials of Committees

O.M.ProvisoExceptionExplanation
Source L1577–1615
Exact-Source Fact Card

The Department of Expenditure vide O.M. No. 19030/1/2017-E.IV dated 14.09.2017 regulated the TA/DA entitlements of non-officials nominated to various government Committees, Boards, Panels, or Inquiry Commissions. The entitlements are graded into three clear categories:

  • Category I: Retired Government Officials:
    Their TA/DA entitlements are the same as their entitlement at the time of retirement based on the revised 7th CPC rates.
  • Category II: Persons from various fields (Non-Officials):
    Their TA/DA is regulated on par with officers in Pay Level 11 in the Pay Matrix:
    • Travel: Economy Class by Air, AC-II Class by Train.
    • Hotel stay: Up to ₹2,812.50 per day.
    • Local Travel within city: Up to ₹422.50 per day.
    • Food lump sum: Not exceeding ₹1,125 per day.
  • Category III: Eminent Personalities (Non-Officials):
    Their TA/DA is regulated on par with officers in Pay Level 14 (pre-revised Grade Pay ₹10,000):
    • Travel: Economy Class by Air, AC-I Class by Train.
    • The Executive Class Exception: The Secretary of the Administrative Ministry, in consultation with the Financial Advisor (FA), may allow them to travel in Executive Class in domestic flights, provided it is their customary mode of travel in non-government affairs, or they were entitled to it before retirement.
    • Hotel stay: Up to ₹9,375 per day.
    • Local Travel within city: AC Taxi charges as per actual expenditure commensurate with official tasks.
    • Food lump sum: Not exceeding ₹1,500 per day.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Committees ke Non-Officials ka TA/DA — 3 Categories (O.M. 14.09.2017): Jab sarkar kisi committee, board, panel, ya inquiry commission banati hai, to usme aksar non-official members bhi hote hain — retired officers, field experts, ya eminent personalities. In logo ka TA/DA kaise regulate hota hai? 3 clear categories hain:
  • Category I — Retired Government Officials: Agar koi retired IAS/IPS/IFS officer ya koi bhi retired sarkari employee committee mein member hai, to uska TA/DA bilkul wahi hoga jo retirement ke time pe tha — lekin 7th CPC ke revised (current) rates pe. Example: Agar wo retirement pe Level 13 tha, to ab bhi Level 13 ki facilities milengi — Economy flight, AC-I train, hotel ₹5,625/day (current), food ₹1,250/day (current). Simple logic: "Jo tha wahi milega — naye rates pe."
  • Category II — General Non-Officials (Field Experts): Agar koi private sector ka expert, academician, ya professional committee mein hai, to use Pay Level 11 ke barabar maana jaata hai. Matlab: Economy class flight, AC-II train, hotel up to ₹2,812.50/day (current), local travel up to ₹422.50/day (current), food lump sum ₹1,125/day (current). Ye sabse common category hai — zyada tar non-official members isme aate hain.
  • Category III — Eminent Personalities (VIPs/Celebrities): Agar koi bohot bade expert, celebrity, ya internationally recognized personality committee mein hai, to use Pay Level 14 ke barabar maana jaata hai. Matlab: Economy flight, AC-I train, hotel up to ₹9,375/day (current), local AC taxi actuals, food ₹1,500/day (current). Ek special exception bhi hai: Secretary of the Administrative Ministry + Financial Advisor (FA) ki joint approval se, inko domestic flights mein Executive Class bhi allow ki ja sakti hai — agar ye unka normal/customary travel mode hai (jaise koi bade businessman jo hamesha Business Class mein travel karta hai).
  • Exam angle: "A retired Level 12 officer is nominated to a government committee. What TA/DA is he entitled to?" → Answer: Same as Level 12 at current 7th CPC rates — Economy flight, AC-I train, hotel ₹5,625/day, food ₹1,250/day. NOT Level 11, NOT Level 14 — same as retirement level.
Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

OM 2 (14.09.2017): non-officials on committees — Cat I retired officials: as at retirement; Cat II: par with Level 11 (Economy air, AC-II, hotel ₹2,812.50, local ₹422.50, food ₹1,125); Cat III eminent: par with Level 14 (Economy air, AC-I; Executive Class exception via Secretary + FA if customary/entitled earlier; hotel ₹9,375; food ₹1,500).

Official-text check · read from the official PDF on 26.11.2026

Official file number is 19047/1/2016-E.IV and the amounts are the 2017 base figures — E4. Local non-officials: only Mileage Allowance, no TA/DA (para 3).

ch07-008

Reference — status variesOM 3: DoE O.M. dated 01.02.2018 — Daily Allowance under Free Boarding and Lodging

O.M.ProvisoExceptionExplanation
Source L1616–16422 superseded lines hidden — use “Show superseded”
Exact-Source Fact Card

Consequent upon the restructured Daily Allowance (DA) components under the 7th CPC (Hotel, Food, Local Travel), Department of Expenditure vide O.M. No. 19030/1/2017-E.IV dated 01.02.2018 clarified the admissibility of Daily Allowance when free boarding and/or lodging is provided by the Government or host organization:

  • Component-wise Zeroing Rule: Since DA is purely a reimbursement of actual expenses, if any component is provided free, that specific claim is zeroed out:
    • Free stay (Lodging) provided: Hotel stay component is reduced to NIL.
    • Free meals (Boarding) provided: Food bill component is reduced to NIL.
    • Both stay and meals provided free: Both Hotel and Food components are reduced to NIL. No Daily Allowance for stay/meals can be claimed.
  • The 25% Flat DA Abolition: Under 5th & 6th CPC, if both boarding and lodging were free, employees were paid a flat 25% of the single-rate DA for out-of-pocket expenses. This O.M. completely abolished the 25% flat DA rule.
  • Local Travel Admissibility: Even if stay and meals are free, the employee is still entitled to claim actual or capped local travelling charges under Para 2E(i) & (iii) for local journeys actually performed on official duty (e.g. from airport to the guest house).
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Free Boarding/Lodging pe kya hoga DA ka? (O.M. 01.02.2018): Ye bohot practical situation hai — jab aap kisi sarkari training ya conference mein jaate ho jahan rehna aur khana dono free hai (jaise LBSNAA Mussoorie ya any sarkari training institute).
  • Purana rule (6th CPC): Pehle agar stay aur food dono free the, to employee ko flat 25% DA milta tha "out-of-pocket expenses" ke naam pe. Matlab agar aapka DA ₹1,000/day tha, to ₹250 milta tha chahe aapka kuch kharcha nahi hua.
  • Naya rule (7th CPC, O.M. 01.02.2018): Ab ye 25% flat DA completely band ho gaya hai! Naya system component-wise hai: (1) Agar stay free hai → Hotel component = NIL (zero). (2) Agar food free hai → Food component = NIL (zero). (3) Agar local transport free hai (jaise official gaadi) → Travel component = NIL. (4) Agar dono stay aur food free hain → dono NIL — sirf local travel ka actual kharcha claim kar sakte ho (jaise airport se guest house tak ka auto).
  • Example: Level 9 officer training pe gaya jahan hostel + mess dono free hain. Uska normal DA hota: Hotel ₹2,812.50 + Food ₹1,125 + Travel ₹422.50 = ₹4,360/day. Ab kya milega? Hotel = NIL, Food = NIL, Travel = ₹422.50 (agar actually use kiya) = sirf ₹422.50/day. Purana 25% rule hota to ₹1,090 milta — ab kam milta hai!
RED-FLAG TRAP

Flag: "If both boarding and lodging are free, a 25% Daily Allowance is paid to the employee."
Fix: This is an outdated 6th CPC rule. Under the latest 01.02.2018 O.M., the 25% flat DA is completely discontinued. Hotel and Food components are reduced to NIL, leaving only local city travel admissible under Para 2E. Fix-phrase: "Free stay + food = Zero stay/food DA; only local travel."

Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

OM 3 (01.02.2018): free lodging → hotel NIL; free meals → food NIL; both free → both NIL; flat 25% DA abolished; local travel under Para 2E(i)/(iii) still admissible.

Study aid · Memory hook

“Free stay + food = Zero stay/food DA; only local travel.” (source fix-phrase)

ch07-009

Reference — status variesOM 4: DoE O.M. dated 13.03.2018 & 15.06.2021 — TA Claim Time Limits

O.M.ExceptionExplanation
Source L1643–1673
Exact-Source Fact Card

To align Travelling Allowance claims with the procedural discipline of the General Financial Rules (GFR) 2017, the submission timelines were drastically modified:

  • 1. Tour, Transfer, and Training claims (O.M. No. 19030/1/2017-E.IV dated 13.03.2018):
    • The ordinary tour/transfer/training claim window is 60 days succeeding the applicable trigger, whether or not an advance was drawn. For the journey itself the trigger is completion; para 2 specifies separate triggers for relevant family journeys and actual delivery/arrival of personal effects or conveyances.
    • This includes road mileage and the transport of personal effects/conveyances (which must be submitted within 60 days of their actual delivery/arrival at the destination).
    • Relinquishment / delayed-claim handling: The O.M. says an unpreferred claim is forfeited/deemed relinquished at the 60-day limit. Para 5 separately says that a TA claim allowed to remain in abeyance for more than one year should be investigated by the Head of Department; if genuineness and valid reasons for delay are established, it may be paid after usual checks. This is a limited stated procedure—not automatic or general condonation for any late claim.
  • 2. TA on Retirement claims (O.M. No. 19030/1/2017-E.IV dated 15.06.2021):
    • Recognizing the practical disruptions and settlement delays faced by retired employees and their families, the time limit for submitting Retirement TA claims was relaxed to 180 days (6 months) succeeding the date of completion of the journey.
    • This 180-day window applies only to retirement claims; ordinary tour, transfer and training claims remain due within 60 days of the applicable trigger, whether or not an advance was drawn.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Bill jama karne ki deadlines — 3 important numbers: Ye bohot practical aur commonly tested topic hai:
  • 1. Tour/Transfer/Training ka bill — 60 din (O.M. 13.03.2018): Deadline is 60 days succeeding completion of the journey, whether or not a TA advance was drawn. A late claim is treated as forfeited/deemed relinquished under the O.M.; para 5 is a specific HOD review route for a claim kept in abeyance for more than one year, not a blanket extension for any valid reason. Retirement TA has its separate 180-day order.
  • 2. Retirement TA ka bill — 180 din (O.M. 15.06.2021): Retired employees ke liye deadline 3 guna zyada — 180 din (6 mahine) hai. Reason: Retirement ke baad settlement mein time lagta hai — naya ghar dhundho, bank accounts transfer karo, family ko relocate karo. 15 June 2021 ko ye relaxation specifically retirement claims ke liye di gayi.
  • TA advance adjustment: The inspected 2005 Compendium states a 15-day tour-advance adjustment period (currency unverified); its Rule 43 transfer-advance text gives no general 30-day deadline. Check the current departmental order.
  • Comparison: The ordinary 60-day TA claim-submission rule applies whether or not an advance was drawn; it is distinct from advance adjustment. The inspected 2005 Compendium says 15 days for tour advances, but its currency is unverified and no general 30-day transfer deadline was located.
Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

OM 4: 13.03.2018 → 60 days succeeding trigger (advance or not); unpreferred claim deemed relinquished; Para 5: claim in abeyance >1 year investigated by HoD, payable only if genuineness/valid delay shown. 15.06.2021 → retirement TA 180 days.

ch07-010

Reference — status variesOM 5: DoE O.M. dated 29.06.2018 — GST/Taxes on Hotel Entitlement

O.M.Explanation
Source L1674–1700
Exact-Source Fact Card

Following the introduction of the Goods and Services Tax (GST) in July 2017, numerous references were made to the Ministry of Finance regarding whether hotel tax is inclusive of or exclusive of the pay-level-based hotel accommodation ceilings.

The Rule (DoE O.M. No. 19030/2/2017-E.IV dated 29.06.2018):

  • GST is Exclusive: The hotel staying accommodation ceilings prescribed under the revised TA Rules (Para 2E(i) of the 13.07.2017 O.M.) are strictly exclusive of all taxes and GST.
  • Reimbursement Over and Above the Ceiling: The GST/taxes levied by hotels shall be reimbursed to the Government servant over and above the prescribed ceiling/entitlement!
  • The Proportionate Restriction: The reimbursement of GST/taxes is calculated only on the actual room tariff paid within their prescribed ceiling.
    • Case A (Within Ceiling): A PML 9 officer (entitled up to ₹2,812.50/day) stays in a room costing ₹2,500/night with ₹450 GST. Since ₹2,500 is within the ceiling, they get reimbursed the full ₹2,500 + ₹450 GST = ₹2,950.
    • Case B (Exceeding Ceiling): The same officer stays in a room costing ₹3,500/night with ₹630 GST. Their room rent is restricted to the ceiling of ₹2,812.50, and their GST reimbursement is restricted to the GST rate calculated on ₹2,812.50 (e.g. ₹506.25 GST), resulting in ₹2,812.50 + ₹506.25 = ₹3,318.75. The remaining excess tariff and excess GST must be borne personally.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Hotel pe GST ka paisa alag se milta hai! (O.M. 29.06.2018): Ye bohot employee-friendly rule hai. Hotel ke room rent ke upar jo GST (Goods and Services Tax) lagta hai (usually 12% ya 18%), wo aapki hotel ceiling ke alag se (over and above) reimburse hota hai.
  • Case A — Room rent ceiling ke andar: Level 9 officer (ceiling ₹2,812.50) ne ₹2,500 ka room liya + ₹450 GST. Total bill = ₹2,950. Kya milega? Poora ₹2,950 milega! Room rent (₹2,500) ceiling ke andar hai, to GST (₹450) bhi poora milta hai — ceiling se upar jaake bhi!
  • Case B — Room rent ceiling se upar: Wahi Level 9 officer ne ₹3,500 ka room liya + ₹630 GST. Total bill = ₹4,130. Kya milega? Room rent ceiling pe restrict hoga: ₹2,812.50. GST bhi sirf ceiling pe calculate hoga: 18% of ₹2,812.50 = ₹506.25. Total = ₹2,812.50 + ₹506.25 = ₹3,318.75. Baaki ₹811.25 aapko apni jeb se dena hoga.
  • Key insight: Ceilings strictly exclusive of GST hain — ye exam ka favourite calculation trap hai. Jab question mein hotel rent + GST diya ho, to pehle dekho rent ceiling ke andar hai ya nahi — phir GST accordingly calculate karo.
Exam Angle / PYQ

A high-yield MCQ calculation trap! "A Level 9 officer stays in a hotel costing ₹2,500/night + ₹450 GST. Their entitlement ceiling is ₹2,812.50. How much is reimbursed?" — many candidates restrict the total to the ₹2,812.50 ceiling. The correct answer is ₹2,950 (₹2,500 actual rent + ₹450 GST), because GST is paid over and above the ceiling when the room tariff is within the limit.

Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

OM 5 (29.06.2018): hotel ceilings exclusive of GST; GST reimbursed over the ceiling but only on tariff within entitlement. Level 9: ₹2,500 + ₹450 GST = ₹2,950; ₹3,500 room → ₹2,812.50 + ₹506.25 = ₹3,318.75.

ch07-011

Primary source checkedDoE O.M. dated 23.06.2020 — Boarding Pass with TA Bills

O.M.Explanation
Source L1701–1719
Official DoE O.M. No. 19030/3/2014-E.IV

The O.M. states that when a Central Government employee is unable to submit the boarding pass along with the TA bill, the employee may submit the prescribed self-declaration certificate in lieu. The annexed proforma includes journey/PNR and actual-travel details and a no-double-claim certification.

  • Counter-signature: the proforma is to be countersigned by the Controlling Officer for officials below Under Secretary level (Pay Level 10 and below), as stated in the O.M.
  • Scope: these instructions apply to journeys on tour performed by Central Government employees.
  • Do not add unsupported steps: the retrieved O.M. does not require a separate airline “Certificate of Travel” or prescribe a fee/penalty for a missing boarding pass. Check any other applicable instruction before acting on a claim.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Boarding pass submit nahi kar sakte? Central Government tour TA bill ke saath pass dena mumkin na ho, to 23.06.2020 O.M. ka prescribed self-declaration in lieu submit kiya ja sakta hai.
  • Counter-sign: Pay Level 10 aur neeche ke stated group ke liye Controlling Officer ki countersignature chahiye; annexed proforma follow karo.
  • Important: Is O.M. mein airline ka alag certificate ya ₹100–₹300 fee/penalty nahi likha.
Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

23.06.2020 (19030/3/2014-E.IV): missing boarding pass → prescribed self-declaration; countersigned by Controlling Officer for Level 10 and below; tour journeys only; no airline certificate/fee requirement added.

ch07-012

Primary source checkedDoE O.M. dated 22.12.2020 — City Travel Receipts/Vouchers under DA on Tour

O.M.ProvisoExplanation
Source L1720–1739
Official DoE O.M. No. 19030/2/2020-E.IV

The 13.07.2017 base TA O.M. allowed Pay Levels 8 and below to claim eligible tour local-travel charges under DA without vouchers against self-certification. The 22.12.2020 O.M. removes the receipt/voucher condition for Pay Levels 9–11, subject to a self-certification stating the period of travel and vehicle number.

  • What changed: documentation for Level 9–11 local-travel claims. The O.M. does not change their pay-level travel mode, local-travel ceiling, or create a disability-specific AC-taxi entitlement.
  • Current rate: apply the operative level ceiling in the 2017 TA O.M. as adjusted by applicable rate revisions; the 2020 O.M. itself does not set a new ceiling.
  • Pay Levels 12 and above: the 2020 O.M. does not extend this documentation waiver to them. Apply the base order and any other applicable instruction.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Level 9–11: 22.12.2020 O.M. ke baad local tour-travel claim mein receipt/voucher ke badle self-certification chal sakta hai; period of travel aur vehicle number dena hai.
  • Dhyan rahe: Ye documentation change hai — taxi class, ceiling ya Divyang-specific local-AC-taxi entitlement ka rule nahi.
  • Level 12+: Is O.M. ne in levels ko waiver nahi diya; base rule aur anya applicable instructions dekho.
Exam angle

The 22.12.2020 order specifically extends the no-voucher/self-certification route to Pay Levels 9–11. Treat it as a proof/documentation change, not as a change to the mode or amount of local-travel entitlement.

Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

22.12.2020 (19030/2/2020-E.IV): Level 9–11 may claim tour local travel without receipts on self-certification of travel period and vehicle number; not a change in mode or ceiling; not extended to Level 12+.

ch07-013

Reference — status variesOM 8: DoE O.M. dated 06.01.2022 — CTG on Retirement

O.M.ProvisoExplanation
Source L1740–17611 superseded line hidden — use “Show superseded”
Exact-Source Fact Card

Under the base O.M. dated 13.07.2017, retired employees settling down at their last station of duty, or within 20 km of it, were restricted to a reduced one-third (1/3rd) Composite Transfer Grant (CTG).

The Revision (DoE O.M. No. 19030/1/2017-E.IV dated 06.01.2022):

  • 20 Km restriction abolished: The 20 km distance restriction for claiming full CTG on retirement is completely done away with / abolished.
  • Full CTG Admissible: Retiring Central Government employees who wish to settle down at their last station of duty, or within 20 km of it, are entitled to FULL CTG at the rate of 80% of their last month's Basic Pay, provided there is an actual change of residence involved.
  • Abolition of 1/3rd CTG: The old one-third (1/3rd) CTG reduction for retirement is completely defunct. If there is an actual change of residence, the retiree gets 100% of their eligible 80% basic pay CTG (e.g., if basic pay is ₹1,00,000, they get the full ₹80,000 CTG). If there is no change of residence, CTG is Nil.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Retirement pe same city mein settle hone pe FULL CTG! (O.M. 06.01.2022): Ye ek revolutionary change hai jo 2022 mein aaya. Pehle kya tha aur ab kya hai:
  • Purana rule (pre-2022): Agar aap retire hokar usi shehar mein settle hote the jahan last posting thi (ya 20 km ke andar), to CTG kat ke sirf 1/3rd (one-third) milti thi. Matlab agar aapka normal CTG ₹80,000 banta tha (80% of basic), to sirf ₹26,667 milta. Reason: "Same city mein to move karna easy hai, kam kharcha hoga."
  • Naya rule (post 06.01.2022): Ye 1/3rd restriction completely abolish ho gayi! Ab agar aap retire hokar same city mein settle hote ho — bas actual ghar badla (change of residence) hona chahiye — to FULL CTG (80% of basic pay) milegi! Example: Basic pay ₹1,00,000 → CTG = 80% = ₹80,000 — pehle sirf ₹26,667 milta, ab poora ₹80,000 milega.
  • Condition: Sirf ek cheez zaroori hai — actual change of residence. Agar aap usi ghar mein reh rahe ho (ghar nahi badla), to CTG = NIL. Ek self-declaration deni hogi (Annexure-I format mein) ki "Maine ghar badla hai."
  • Transfer vs Retirement CTG comparison: Transfer pe same city mein (with residence change) = 1/3rd CTG (26.67%). Retirement pe same city mein (with residence change) = FULL CTG (80%). Ye difference exams mein bohot puchha jaata hai!
Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

OM 8 (06.01.2022): retirement CTG — 20-km restriction removed; full 80% if actual change of residence (settling at/within 20 km of last station); no change of residence → nil; one-third reduction defunct for retirement.

ch07-014

Reference — status variesOM 9: DoE O.M. dated 24.01.2022 — TA for Re-employed Persons

O.M.Explanation
Source L1762–1796
Exact-Source Fact Card

To regulate Travelling Allowance (self/family fares, CTG, personal effects, and conveyance transport) for retired persons who are re-employed in Central Government service, Department of Expenditure vide O.M. No. 19030/4/2020-E.IV dated 24.01.2022 issued consolidated guidelines:

  • Scenario I: Pensioner who HAS already claimed TA on Retirement:
    If they are subsequently re-employed at a station other than their place of settlement, or if the re-employment necessitates a change of residence:
    • They are allowed Transfer TA for joining the re-employed post, and also upon completion of their re-employment term.
    • Cost-bearing: In both cases, the Travelling Allowance is borne and reimbursed by the borrowing organization (the office where they are re-employed).
    • Entitlement scale: Follows the pay scale of the re-employed post.
  • Scenario II: Pensioner who HAS NOT claimed TA on Retirement:
    If they are re-employed within one year of retirement and have not yet claimed their retirement TA:
    • They are allowed TA for joining the re-employed post (if at a station other than their last station of duty, or if it necessitates a change of residence).
    • Who bears the joining cost?: The Travelling Allowance for joining is borne and paid by the organization from which they retired (with reference to the post held at retirement).
    • Who bears the reversion/completion cost?: Upon completion of the re-employment term, the return TA is paid by the re-employing organization.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • "R-A-S" — 3 Special Category Clarifications: Ye teen alag-alag groups ke liye special TA rules hain jo exams mein often puchhe jaate hain:
  • 1. Re-employed Persons (O.M. 24.01.2022): Agar koi retired pensioner dobara sarkari naukri mein re-employed hota hai (contract basis pe, ya specific tenure ke liye), to uska TA kaise handle hoga? Do scenarios hain:
    • Scenario A — Pensioner ne already Retirement TA le liya tha: To re-employment joining ka TA aur term khatam hone pe return ka TA — donono re-employing (borrowing) office pay karega. Simple.
    • Scenario B — Pensioner ne abhi tak Retirement TA nahi liya (1 saal ke andar re-employ ho gaye): To joining ka TA purana office (jahan se retire hue) pay karega — retirement pay level ke hisaab se. Aur term khatam hone pe return ka TA naya re-employing office pay karega. Ye thoda complex hai lekin logical hai — retirement ka paisa purana office dega, re-employment ka paisa naya office dega.
  • 2. Attendant/Escort for Disabled (O.M. 17.02.2015): Agar koi Divyang (disabled) employee tour pe ja raha hai aur use escort/sahayak ki zaroorat hai, to escort ka travel fare (same class ticket) reimburse hoga. Lekin dhyan rahe: escort ko koi DA nahi milega — na hotel, na food. Sirf aane-jaane ka ticket. Ye ek bada trap hai exams mein.
  • 3. Sportspersons: Agar koi sarkari employee national/international sports event mein participate kar raha hai (as player, coach, manager, doctor, masseur), to use First Class rail milegi — chahe uska Pay Level kuch bhi ho. Aur agar event abroad hai, to Economy Class air milegi. Ye special concession hai — normally Level 5- wale ko First Class nahi milti.
Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

OM 9 (24.01.2022): re-employed — Scenario I (already claimed retirement TA): transfer TA to join and at end, borne by borrowing organization, scale of re-employed post; Scenario II (not claimed, re-employed within 1 year): joining TA borne by organization retired from; return TA by re-employing organization.

Official-text check · read from the official PDF on 26.11.2026

Official para 2(i) bases the entitlement on the post last held and last pay drawn at retirement — E5.

ch07-015

Reference — status variesOM 10: DoE O.M. dated 16.06.2022 — Transfer TA to/from NER, A&N, Lakshadweep, and Ladakh

O.M.Explanation
Source L1797–1819
Exact-Source Fact Card

Under the base TA Rules (13.07.2017), the production of receipts/vouchers for the transportation of personal effects was made mandatory for transfers to/from the North-Eastern Region (NER), Andaman & Nicobar, Lakshadweep, and Ladakh. Department of Expenditure vide O.M. No. 19030/1/2017-E.IV dated 16.06.2022 modified the condition as follows:

  • 1. If the family DOES NOT accompany the employee on transfer:
    The employee is entitled to carry personal effects up to one-third (1/3rd) of their normal weight entitlement (e.g. 2,000 kg out of 6,000 kg for Level 6+).
    The Voucher relaxation: The production of actual receipts/vouchers is NOT mandatory to claim this 1/3rd entitlement for transportation of personal effects. It can be claimed based on Self-Certification.
  • 2. If the family DOES accompany the employee on transfer:
    The employee is entitled to their full weight entitlement.
    The Voucher mandate: The production of actual receipts/vouchers remains strictly mandatory to claim the full cost of transportation.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Special Regions — NE, A&N, Lakshadweep, Ladakh — ke transfer rules (O.M. 16.06.2022): In areas mein transfer special hai kyunki wahan transport options limited hain aur vouchers milna mushkil hota hai (jaise Ladakh mein truck wala receipt nahi deta). Isliye sarkar ne relaxed rules banaye hain.
  • Scenario 1 — Family sath nahi ja rahi: Agar aap akele ja rahe ho (family baad mein aayegi ya nahi aayegi), to aap apne total weight entitlement ka 1/3rd saman le ja sakte ho — aur iska claim bina kisi voucher ke, sirf self-certification pe kar sakte ho! Example: Level 9 officer (6,000 kg entitled) → 1/3 = 2,000 kg ka transport cost claim kar sakte ho bina bill ke. Reason: Wahan transport operators proper bills nahi dete, to sarkar ne self-certification accept kar liya.
  • Scenario 2 — Family sath ja rahi hai: Agar poori family sath ja rahi hai, to aap poora weight entitlement le ja sakte ho (e.g., 6,000 kg for Level 6+). Lekin is case mein original transport bills/vouchers dena compulsory hai — self-certification nahi chalega. Reason: Jab family sath hai to aap proper packers & movers use karoge, aur wo bill dete hain.
  • TA entitlement basis on transfer — "Lower of Two Posts": Transfer pe aapki TA entitlement purani post aur nayi post mein se jo junior (lower pay) hai uske hisaab se milti hai. Example: Agar aap Level 10 se Level 8 pe transfer hue (demotion ya functional change), to TA Level 8 ke hisaab se milega. Lekin agar Level 8 se Level 10 pe promotion hua, to bhi TA Level 8 ke hisaab se milega (lower of the two). Ye rule "no windfall gain" ke liye hai.
Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

OM 10 (16.06.2022): to/from NER, A&N, Lakshadweep, Ladakh — family not accompanying: up to 1/3 weight entitlement, self-certification (no vouchers); family accompanying: full entitlement, vouchers mandatory.

ch07-016

Reference — status variesOM 11: DoE O.M. dated 12.09.2022 — Travel by Tejas Express Trains on Tour

O.M.Explanation
Source L1820–1841
Exact-Source Fact Card

Department of Expenditure O.M. No. 19030/1/2017-E.IV dated 12.09.2022 allows travel by Tejas Express on journeys on tour, training, transfer and retirement. Its class entitlement is the same as the Shatabdi provision in Para 2A(ii) of the 13.07.2017 O.M.; check the employee’s pay level against that provision.

Pay Matrix LevelTejas Express Entitled Class
Pay Level 12 and aboveExecutive Class (dynamic-fare) / AC Chair Car
Pay Level 6 to 11AC Chair Car
Pay Level 5 and belowAC Chair Car
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Premium Trains kya hoti hain? Indian Railways ne kai "premium" category trains chalu ki hain jaise Rajdhani, Shatabdi, Duronto, Suvidha, Premium Tatkal, aur Tejas Express. In trains mein normal trains se alag pricing hoti hai — kuch mein dynamic fare (jaise Uber ka surge pricing — demand ke hisaab se price badhta hai) aur kuch mein Premium Tatkal (jisme last-minute booking ke liye bahut zyada charge lagta hai). Pehle confusion tha ki kya sarkari employees in mehngi trains mein travel kar sakte hain aur kya full reimbursement milega.
  • Rule kya hai? Haan — aap in sab premium trains mein travel kar sakte ho aur jo bhi dynamic/surge fare lagega, wo fully reimburse hoga — koi cap nahi hai! Lekin ek condition: aapko apni pay level ke according class mein hi travel karna hai. Example: Agar aap Level 9 ho (AC-II entitled), to Rajdhani mein AC-II Tier hi lena hai — AC-I nahi le sakte chahe kitna bhi zaroori kyun na ho. Same logic Tejas Express pe bhi apply hota hai.
  • Tejas Express (12 Sept 2022 ka update): Tejas Express ek relatively nayi train category hai (IRCTC dwara operated). 12 September 2022 tak iska TA Rules mein koi explicit mention nahi tha. Is O.M. ne clear kar diya: Level 12+ → Executive Class, Level 6-11 → AC Chair Car, Level 5 and below → AC Chair Car. Tejas mein dynamic fare lagta hai — wo bhi reimburse hoga.
  • Tatkal vs Premium Tatkal — dono allowed hain: Tatkal (fixed extra charge, jaise ₹200-400 extra) bhi reimburse hota hai, aur Premium Tatkal (dynamic, kabhi ₹2,000-5,000 extra bhi ho sakta hai) bhi reimburse hota hai. Exam trap: "Premium Tatkal charge reimbursable nahi hai" — ye galat statement hai, dono reimbursable hain.
  • Analogy: Socho jaise aap official kaam se Uber/Ola lete ho — agar surge pricing hai (2x, 3x) to bhi office poora reimburse karta hai kyunki aapke paas koi choice nahi tha. Same logic yahan apply hota hai — Rajdhani mein dynamic fare lage to bhi sarkar poora degi.
Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

OM 11 (12.09.2022): Tejas allowed for tour, training, transfer, retirement; class per Shatabdi Para 2A(ii): Level 12+ Executive/AC Chair Car; 6–11 and 5 & below AC Chair Car (as tabulated).

Class table: see E10.

ch07-017

Reference — status variesOM 12: Current DoE Air-Travel Booking Instructions (31.12.2021, 16.02.2022, 16.06.2022)

O.M.ProvisoExplanation
Source L1842–18661 superseded line hidden — use “Show superseded”
Exact-Source Fact Card

Following the privatization and disinvestment of Air India, the Department of Expenditure consolidated and modified the air travel instructions on Government account through three critical O.Ms:

  • 1. DoE O.M. No. 19024/03/2021-E.IV dated 31.12.2021 (Air India Disinvestment Trigger):
    With the disinvestment of Air India, the old monopoly restriction of "Air India only" was terminated. For all air travel funded by the Government, tickets must strictly be purchased only through the 3 Authorized Travel Agents (ATAs): (1) Balmer Lawrie & Co., (2) Ashok Travels & Tours, and (3) IRCTC.
  • 2. DoE O.M. of even number dated 16.02.2022 (Guidelines on Best Available Fare):
    Introduced the 3-hour time band slot and the concept of "Best Available Fare" (the cheapest available fare in the relevant fixed three-hour time band, preferably non-stop). Retain the ATA fare-page printout as required by the order.
  • 3. DoE O.M. of even number dated 16.06.2022 (Abolition of Agency Fees & Modifications):
    Clarified that the choice of booking agent from the 3 ATAs is left open to the employee/department. No booking fees or convenience charges can be paid to these ATAs. Air tickets booked outside the three ATAs are not reimbursable unless an authorized relaxation is granted under the applicable order. These are air-ticket instructions; do not generalize them to every rail-ticket purchase.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Air Ticket Booking — 3 Authorized Agents (ATAs) se hi book karo! (31.12.2021, 16.02.2022, 16.06.2022):
  • Kyun change hua? Pehle Air India sarkari thi — to rule tha "sirf Air India se book karo." Jab Air India ko Tata Group ko bech diya (disinvestment, late 2021), to ye rule khatam hua. Ab kisi bhi airline ki flight le sakte ho — lekin booking sirf 3 authorized agents se karni hai:
  • 3 ATAs: (1) Balmer Lawrie & Co. (govemp.balmerlawrietravelapp.com), (2) Ashok Travels & Tours (ATT) / ITDC (attitdc.in), (3) IRCTC (air.irctc.co.in). In teeno mein se kisi se bhi book karo — choice aapki hai.
  • Best Available Fare Rule (16.02.2022): Booking karte waqt, aapke travel time ke 3-hour time band mein jo sabse sasti direct flight available hai — wahi leni hai. Example: For a 10:00 AM departure, compare flights in the listed 09:00–12:00 time band; prefer non-stop where available and retain the relevant ATA fare printout. Separately, DoPT O.M. 29.08.2022 encourages at least 21 days’ lead time for LTC air booking; that is not a compulsory claim deadline.
  • ⚠ Air-ticket channel: Government-account air tickets booked outside the required ATA channel may be disallowed unless an authorized relaxation is granted for unavoidable circumstances. Follow the applicable order and competent-authority process; this is not an ATA-only rule for every rail ticket.
  • No booking charges: Teeno ATAs ko koi extra booking fee/convenience charge nahi dena hai — ye sarkar ne 16.06.2022 ko clarify kiya.
Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

OM 12 (air): 31.12.2021 — three ATAs only (Balmer Lawrie, Ashok Travels & Tours, IRCTC); 16.02.2022 — best available fare in 3-hour band; 16.06.2022 — free choice among 3 ATAs, no fees, outside-ATA tickets not reimbursable absent relaxation; air only, not rail.

ch07-018

Reference — status variesOM 13: DoPT JCM TA/DA Rules & DoPT LTC Orders

O.M.OrderProvisoExceptionExplanation
Source L1867–1901
Exact-Source Fact Card

TA, LTC, and JCM rules are governed by DoPT, keeping their core provisions aligned with the central Travelling Allowance structure:

  • 1. DoPT JCM TA/DA Rules (O.M. No. 31011/10/2017-Estt.(A-IV) dated 31.07.2018):
    Under JCM (Joint Consultative Machinery) guidelines, the travel entitlements of Staff Side members are aligned with central officers:
    • National Council members: Entitled to TA/DA on par with Pay Levels 12 and 13 (Economy Air, AC-I Class train, hotel stay up to ₹5,625/day).
    • Departmental/Regional Council members: Entitled on par with Pay Level 11 (Economy Air, AC-II Class train, hotel up to ₹2,812.50/day).
  • 2. DoPT LTC orders (19.09.2017 & 30.04.2024):
    LTC travel entitlements are kept identical to tour entitlements with three major exceptions:
    • Different normal air entitlement on LTC: Pay Levels 6 to 8 have a different normal LTC mode entitlement from official tour/transfer. Check CCS (LTC) Rule 12 and any current special-dispensation or later order before deciding the mode.
    • No Daily Allowance on LTC: No hotel, meals, or city travelling charges are reimbursed.
    • Transport on LTC: Follow the CCS (LTC) mode rule and its stated exceptions. Do not state “government vehicles only” without the applicable exception and order; check the Divyang and unconnected-stretch provisions separately.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • JCM Staff Side members ka TA/DA: JCM (Joint Consultative Machinery) sarkari employees ki representative body hai. Jab JCM meetings hoti hain, to Staff Side members ko TA/DA milta hai — lekin kitna? National Council members → Level 12-13 ke barabar (Economy air, AC-I train, hotel ₹5,625/day). Departmental/Regional Council members → Level 11 ke barabar (Economy air, AC-II train, hotel ₹2,812.50/day).
  • LTC ke 3 bade gaps (vs Tour): LTC (Leave Travel Concession) pe entitlements almost same hain tour jaisi — lekin 3 major exceptions hain jo exams mein bohot test hoti hain:
    1. Level 6-8 ko flight nahi milti LTC pe! Tour pe ye log Economy flight le sakte hain, lekin LTC pe sirf AC-II Tier train milega (special dispensation ko chhod ke — J&K, NER, Ladakh, A&N).
    2. LTC pe koi DA nahi milta — na hotel, na food, na local taxi. Sirf aane-jaane ka ticket fare.
    3. Private taxi on LTC: Apply the authorized-mode rule and the specific exceptions. The disability exception is conditional (medical certificate, undertaking, actual own/hired-vehicle journey, and shortest entitled rail/air fare cap); it is not a general local-tour taxi entitlement. The unconnected-stretch exception is separate and limited to the applicable order’s 100 km each-side cap.
Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

OM 13: JCM (31.07.2018) — National Council par with Levels 12–13; Departmental/Regional Councils par with Level 11. LTC = tour entitlement with 3 exceptions (different air entitlement for L6–8, no DA, transport mode rule).

ch07-019

Reference — status variesTA & Pay Responsibilities during Deputation, Foreign Service, and Joining Time under CCS Rules

ExceptionExplanation
Source L1902–1941
Exact-Source Fact Card

Under the Central Civil Services (CCS) rules — specifically the CCS (Joining Time) Rules, 1979 (Rule 7), standard DoPT Deputation Guidelines (OM No. 6/8/2009-Estt.(Pay-II)), and Supplementary Rules (SR 206) — the financial responsibility for Travelling Allowance (TA), transit pay, and joining time pay is regulated with absolute procedural clarity:

  • 1. During Deputation (Within Central Government / PSUs / Autonomous Bodies):
    • Onward Journey (Joining Deputation): The Transfer TA (including fares, CTG, and transportation of personal effects/conveyance) is borne and paid entirely by the borrowing department/organization.
    • Return Journey (Reversion to Parent Cadre): Upon repatriation/reversion, the return Transfer TA is also borne and paid entirely by the borrowing department/organization (the organization they are leaving).
    • On-Tour TA/DA: All Travelling Allowance and Daily Allowance for official tours performed during the deputation tenure are borne and sanctioned by the borrowing department/organization.
    • The Absorption Exception: If the employee is permanently absorbed into the borrowing organization, NO return TA is payable, as no repatriation journey takes place.
  • 2. During Foreign Service (Transfer to State Govts / International Bodies / PSUs on Foreign Terms):
    • Onward & Return Journeys: Under SR 206, both the onward Transfer TA (to join) and the return Transfer TA (on reversion) are borne and paid entirely by the foreign employer.
    • On-Tour TA/DA: All TA/DA for official tours performed during the foreign service period are paid entirely by the foreign employer, governed by the foreign employer's rules (or parent-cadre rules if specified in the terms of deputation).
  • 3. Joining Time Pay & Salary Responsibility (Rule 7, CCS (Joining Time) Rules, 1979):
    • Core Mandate: A Government servant on joining time is regarded as on duty and is entitled to Joining Time Pay (equal to the pay drawn in the old post before relinquishing charge, along with DA, HRA, and CCA appropriate to the old station).
    • Who Pays for Joining Time?: Under Rule 7, the joining time pay and allowances shall be paid for strictly by the new administrative authority (the borrowing department / new organization) where the Government servant joins on transfer.
    • On Reversion: Upon repatriation/reversion back to the parent cadre, the borrowing organization (the department being left) bears the joining time pay, allowances, and transit pay for the return journey.
Simple Terms (Hinglish)
  • Deputation pe TA kaun deta hai? Simple rule: "The Guest Never Pays!"
  • Deputation (within Central Govt/PSUs): Jab aap apne parent department se kisi dusre department/PSU/autonomous body mein deputation pe jaate ho, to aane-jaane ka poora Transfer TA borrowing department pay karega — dono directions mein! Joining ka bhi, reversion (wapas aane) ka bhi. Aur deputation ke dauran jo bhi official tours hain — unka TA/DA bhi borrowing department dega.
  • Foreign Service (State Govt/International bodies): SR 206 ke under, foreign employer aane-jaane ka poora TA pay karega. Example: Agar aap World Bank ya UN mein deputation pe ja rahe ho, to World Bank/UN aapka TA dega.
  • Absorption exception: Agar aap deputation ke dauran borrowing department mein permanently absorb ho jaate ho, to return TA nahi milega — kyunki aap wapas ja hi nahi rahe! Aap ab permanently wahin ke ho gaye.
  • Joining Time Pay (Rule 7, CCS Joining Time Rules 1979): Transfer ke dauran jo joining time milta hai (travel days + joining days), uska pay naya administrative office dega — purana nahi. Joining Time Pay = purane post ki pay + DA + HRA. Reversion pe bhi same rule: joining time pay borrowing office dega.
Memory Hook

"The Guest Never Pays. Whether joining or returning from deputation/foreign service, the borrowing/host organization (guest office) bears the Transfer TA and the Joining Time Pay."

Exam Angle / PYQ

Highly targeted exam questions: "Who is responsible for paying joining time pay under Rule 7?" — Option (a) Old office, (b) New office, (c) Shared equally, (d) Personal expense. The correct answer is (b) New office / new administrative authority. Another tested point is reversion return TA: it is always borne by the borrowing department, NOT the parent cadre.

Study aid · Quick revision (not source text)

Deputation/foreign service: borrowing/host organization bears transfer TA (onward and return) and tour TA/DA; absorption → no return TA; Rule 7 CCS (Joining Time) Rules: new authority pays joining time pay.

Study aid · Memory hook

“The Guest Never Pays” — host/borrowing organization bears TA and joining-time pay. (source hook)
